1	# Anime Extra Random Defence
2	
3	โปรเจกต์นี้เป็นเกม Tower Defense แนว Grail War ที่ดัดแปลงระบบ gacha/merge จากแมป Warcraft III ชื่อ **FERD** (Fate Extra Random Defence) มาทำเป็นเว็บเกม 2 รุ่น (2D Canvas และ 3D Three.js) พร้อมชุดเครื่องมือ Python สำหรับ reverse-engineer ข้อมูลจากแมปต้นฉบับ
4

34	   ▼  extract.py — สกัดไฟล์จาก MPQ (มี decryption)
35	   │
36	   ▼  parse_obj.py — แปลง binary object files (w3u/w3a) เป็น JSON
37	   │
38	   ▼  merges.py — ดึงสูตร merge จาก abilities → merges.json (177 สูตร)
39	   │
40	   ▼  gen_data.py — แมปสูตร FERD ลงบนตัวละครอนิเมะต้นฉบับ → gamedata.json
41	   │
42	   ▼  emit_data.py — แปลง gamedata.json เป็น data.js ของเกม 3D
43	   │
44	   ▼  เกมเว็บ (anime-extra-random-defense-3d/)
45	```
46	
47	---
48	
49	## 1. `_ferd/` — เครื่องมือวิเคราะห์แมป FERD
50	
51	ชุดสคริปต์ Python สำหรับสกัดข้อมูลจากแมป Warcraft III ที่เข้ารหัส (MPQ archive) และแปลงเป็นข้อมูลเกมที่ใช้ได้
52	
53	### สคริปต์หลัก (ตามลำดับไปป์ไลน์)
54	
55	| ไฟล์ | หน้าที่ |
56	|------|---------|
57	| `extract.py` | สกัดไฟล์จาก MPQ archive ที่เข้ารหัส — เพิ่ม decryption support ที่ห้อง `mpyq` ไม่มี (PKWARE implode, sector-based encryption, FIX_KEY) และ dump ไฟล์สำคัญ เช่น `war3map.j`, `war3map.w3u`, `war3map.w3a` |
58	| `parse_obj.py` | แปลง binary object files (`w3u` = ยูนิต, `w3a` = อาบิลิตี) เป็น JSON — อ่าน mod list ที่มี type detection (int/float/string) และรองรับโครงสร้างของ w3a ที่มี level+column |
59	| `dumpobj.py` | hex dump โครงสร้าง object file เพื่อ debug |
60	| `analyze.py` | วิเคราะห์ servant units (ID แบบ `-XY#` = base + เกรด) และ ability string fields |
61	| `recipes.py` | ค้นหา abilities ที่อ้างถึง servant unit IDs (ค้นหาสูตร) |
62	| `recipes2.py` | จัดกลุ่ม abilities ตามชื่อเพื่อหาหมวดหมู่ |
63	| `merges.py` | ดึงสูตร `[Merge]` จาก abilities (`acat` = วัตถุดิบ, `atat` = ผลลัพธ์) → `out/merges.json` (177 สูตร) |
64	| `roster.py` | จัดกลุ่ม servants ตาม base code และเกรด แสดง roster ทั้งหมด |
65	| `johab.py` | ตรวจสอ abilities ชื่อ `#` (สูตรพิเศษ) |
66	| `gen_data.py` | แมปสูตร FERD ลงบนตัวละครอนิเมะต้นฉบับ ~45 ตัว — กำหนด class, สีผม/ตา, ประเภทโจมตี, ค่า stat ฐาน และคูณตามเกรด (E→STAR) → `out/gamedata.json` |
67	| `emit_data.py` | แปลง `gamedata.json` เป็น `data.js` ของเกม 3D — เพิ่มคำอธิบาย, flavor text, ระดับความยาก, faction, gambling config, path/slots, และ portrait functions |
68	| `probe.py` | สำรวจไฟล์ WC3 ที่รู้จัก (SLK, txt) ใน archive |
69	
70	### สคริปต์ crack/debug
71	
72	| ไฟล์ | หน้าที่ |
73	|------|---------|
74	| `recover.py` | กู้ MPQ encryption key จาก known plaintext (offset table ที่ทราบค่า) |
75	| `brute.py` | brute-force key candidates สำหรับ offset table |
76	| `brute_sector.py` | brute-force sector decryption key และทดสอบ decompress |
77	| `dbg.py` | debug การ decrypt sector แรกของ listfile |
78	| `dbg2.py` | debug key adjustment สำหรับ `war3map.w3u` |
79	
80	### ไฟล์ใน `out/`
81	
82	| ไฟล์ | คำอธิบาย |
83	|------|---------|
84	| `war3map.j` | JASS script ของแมป |
85	| `war3map.w3u` / `.json` | ข้อมูล custom units (binary + JSON) |
86	| `war3map.w3a` / `.json` | ข้อมูล custom abilities (binary + JSON) |
87	| `war3map.w3i` | ข้อมูลแมป (ชื่อ, ผู้สร้าง, ขนาด) |
88	| `war3map.w3b/w3d/w3h/w3q` | buffs, destructables, doodads, upgrades |
89	| `war3map.wts` | string table |
90	| `war3map.wtg/wct` | trigger data |
91	| `war3mapMisc/Skin/Extra.txt` | gameplay constants |
92	| `merges.json` | 177 สูตร merge (req → result) |
93	| `gamedata.json` | ข้อมูลเกมที่สร้างขึ้น (units, recipes, gacha pool) |
94	
95	### วิธีรัน
96	
97	```bash
98	cd _ferd
99	pip install mpyq
100	py extract.py          # สกัดไฟล์จาก map.mpq → out/
101	py parse_obj.py war3map.w3u   # แปลง units → JSON
102	py parse_obj.py war3map.w3a   # แปลง abilities → JSON
103	py merges.py           # ดึงสูตร merge → out/merges.json
104	py gen_data.py         # สร้าง gamedata.json
105	py emit_data.py        # สร้าง data.js สำหรับเกม 3D
106	```
107	
108	> บน Windows ถ้า `python` ไม่ได้ (เด้ง Microsoft Store) ให้ใช้ `py` แทน
109	
110	---
111	
112	## 2. `anime-extra-random-defense/` — เกมเวอร์ชัน 2D
113	
114	เกม Tower Defense บน Canvas 2D แนว Grail War ที่ผสมระบบ gacha
115	
116	### คุณสมบัติ
117	
118	- **Canvas 2D rendering** — วาดด้วย `requestAnimationFrame` loop พร้อม DPR scaling
119	- **4 เกรด + EX** — C / B / A / S (gacha) และ EX (ascend เท่านั้น)
120	- **21 ยูนิต** — 4 C, 4 B, 5 A, 4 S, 8 EX พร้อม portrait SVG ที่สร้างแบบ procedural
121	- **9 สูตร ascension** — รวม base + materials → EX servant
122	- **3 ระดับความยาก** — Normal / Hard / Hell
123	- **25 waves** — boss ทุก 5 waves, pop cap 10
124	- **Gacha summoning** — 50 gold/ครั้ง, pity 12 ครั้งการันตี S
125	- **ประเภทโจมตี 11 แบบ** — single, aoe, chain, slow, crit, burn, poison, stun, beam, pierce, multi
126	- **Web Audio API** — เอฟเฟกต์เสียง procedural (summon, ascend, shoot, hit, ฯลฯ)
127	- **Codex** — ดูสูตร ascension ทั้งหมดและ unit archive (สไตล์ Discord embed)
128	
129	### วิธีเล่น
130	
131	1. **Summon** — สุ่ม servants ด้วย gold (C/B/A/S ตามน้ำหนัก 44/30/18/8%)
132	2. **Deploy** — เลือก servant บน bench → คลิก platform เพื่อวาง (pop cap จำกัด)
133	3. **Ascend** — รวม base + materials → EX servant (ดูสูตรใน Codex)
134	4. **Wave** — เริ่ม wave ศัตรูเดินวงแหวน 1½ รอบ อยู่ให้รอดครบ 25 waves
135	
136	### คีย์ลัด
137	
138	- `Space` = เริ่ม wave
139	- `G` = summon
140	- `P` = pause
141	- `Esc` = deselect
142	
143	### วิธีรัน
144	
145	เปิด `index.html` ในเบราว์เซอร์โดยตรง (ไม่ต้อง server เพราะใช้ plain `<script>` ไม่มี module) หรือใช้ local server:
146	
147	```bash
148	cd anime-extra-random-defense
149	py -m http.server 8000
150	# เปิด http://localhost:8000
151	```
152	
153	> บน Windows ถ้า `python` ไม่ได้ (เด้ง Microsoft Store) ให้ใช้ `py` แทน
154	
155	---
156	
157	## 3. `anime-extra-random-defense-3d/` — เกมเวอร์ชัน 3D (FERD-aligned)
158	
159	เวอร์ชันขยายที่ใช้ข้อมูลจากแมป FERD ต้นฉบับโดยตรง พร้อม 3D rendering ด้วย Three.js
160	
161	### คุณสมบัติใหม่
162	
163	- **Three.js r186 3D rendering** — WebGL + UnrealBloomPass, PCF shadows, PBR materials, image-based lighting, orbit camera (drag/zoom)
164	- **8 เกรด** — E / D / C / B / A / S / EX / ★(STAR) พร้อม ladder สูตรครบ
165	- **177 สูตร ascension** — แปลจาก FERD map 1:1 ลงบนตัวละครอนิเมะต้นฉบับ
166	- **~160 ยูนิต** — ข้ามทุกเกรด พร้อม 3D figures (robe, head, hair, weapon ตาม class, halo สำหรับ S/EX/STAR)
167	- **Gacha upgrade** — ใช้ spirit (✧) อัปเกรด gacha Lv 1-6 (E→D→C→B→A→S)
168	- **Wisp system (❋)** — ได้ 2 wisps/wave แปลงเป็น gold/spirit/summon ได้
169	- **5 Faction** — Aurel Court / Kitsune Covenant / Steppe Horde / Echo Syndicate / Mooncell Bloc พร้อม passive + active ability และ XP ถาวร (localStorage)
170	- **Gambling** — เสี่ยง gold หรือ servant (payouts/chances ตรงกับ FERD)
171	- **Servant Pickup** — เลือก servant เฉพาะเจาะจงด้วย wisps
172	- **Fusion** — รวม 3 servants เกรดเท่ากัน → 1 สุ่มเกรดถัดไป
173	- **12 ระดับความยาก** — Practice → SE.RA.PH → Sakura Labyrinth → Photonic Abyss → Imaginary Number Space I-IV → Hell Proof I-IV
174	- **สูงสุด 100 waves** — pop cap 18, bench 10 ช่อง
175	- **WebGL fallback** — แสดงข้อความถ้าเบราว์เซอร์ไม่รองรับ WebGL
176	
177	### โครงสร้างไฟล์
178	
179	- **`data.js`** — ข้อมูลเกมทั้งหมด: GRADES (8 เกรด), UNITS (~160), RECIPES (177), GACHA_POOL, DIFFICULTIES (12), FACTIONS (5), GOLD_GAMBLES, SERVANT_GAMBLES, PATH, SLOTS, portrait functions
180	- **`game.js`** — เอนจินเกม: state management, faction system (persistent XP via localStorage), gacha upgrade, wisp economy, gambling, combat (11 attack types), wave spawning, ascension, fusion, pickup, UI sync
181	- **`scene3d.js`** — 3D render layer: arena (floor, ring road, gates, platforms), unit figures (procedural per class), enemies (icosa core + glow + boss cage), projectiles, beams, rings, particles, damage floats, ambient motes, orbit controls, camera shake, resize handling
182	
183	### วิธีรัน
184	
185	เปิด `index.html` ในเบราว์เซอร์ (รองรับ `file://` เพราะใช้ dynamic import กับ import map) หรือใช้ local server:
186	
187	```bash
188	cd anime-extra-random-defense-3d
189	py -m http.server 8000
190	# เปิด http://localhost:8000
191	```
192	
193	> บน Windows ถ้า `python` ไม่ได้ (เด้ง Microsoft Store) ให้ใช้ `py` แทน
194	
195	> ต้องการเบราว์เซอร์ที่รองรับ WebGL และ ES modules
196	
197	---
198

200	
201	| ฟีเจอร์ | 2D | 3D |
202	|---------|----|----|
203	| Rendering | Canvas 2D | Three.js WebGL |
204	| เกรด | C/B/A/S/EX (5) | E/D/C/B/A/S/EX/★ (8) |
205	| ยูนิต | 21 | ~160 |
206	| สูตร ascension | 9 | 177 |
207	| ระดับความยาก | 3 | 12 |
208	| Waves | 25 | สูงสุด 100 |
209	| Pop cap | 10 | 18 |
210	| Gacha upgrade | — | ✅ (spirit) |
211	| Wisp system | — | ✅ |
212	| Faction | — | ✅ (5, persistent XP) |
213	| Gambling | — | ✅ (gold + servant) |
214	| Servant pickup | — | ✅ |
215	| Fusion | — | ✅ |
216	| แหล่งข้อมูล | ดัดแปลงเอง | FERD map 1:1 |

220	## การอ้างอิง
221	
222	- **FERD** = Fate Extra Random Defence — แมป Warcraft III ต้นฉบับ (v3.81, build 8275, Fix)
223	- **MPQ** = Mo'PaQ — รูปแบบ archive ของ Blizzard (เข้ารหัส sector-based)
224	- **JASS** = ภาษา scripting ของ Warcraft III
225	- ตัวละครอนิเมะทั้งหมดเป็นต้นฉบับของโปรเจกต์นี้ ไม่ใช่ตัวละครจาก Fate โดยตรง แต่ได้รับแรงบันดาลใจจากธีม Grail War

7	    id, children: [], style: {}, dataset: {}, _innerHTML: '', textContent: '',

9	    set innerHTML(v) { this._innerHTML = v; this.children.length = 0; },

11	    classList: {
12	      add() {}, remove() {}, toggle() {}, contains() { return false; },
13	    },
14	    appendChild(child) { this.children.push(child); child.parentElement = this; return child; },
15	    prepend(child) { this.children.unshift(child); child.parentElement = this; return child; },
16	    remove() { if (this.parentElement && this.parentElement.children) this.parentElement.children = this.parentElement.children.filter(x => x !== this); },
17	    get lastChild() { return this.children[this.children.length - 1] || null; },
18	    addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {}, setAttribute() {},
19	  };
20	  el.parentElement = { classList: { add() {}, remove() {}, toggle() {} } };
21	  return el;

25	  const elements = new Map();

27	    getElementById(id) { if (!elements.has(id)) elements.set(id, element(id)); return elements.get(id); },
28	    createElement(tag) { return element(tag); },
29	    querySelectorAll() { return []; },
30	    addEventListener() {},
31	  };
32	  const storage = new Map();
33	  const context = {
34	    console, document, elements,
35	    localStorage: { getItem: k => storage.get(k) || null, setItem: (k, v) => storage.set(k, v) },
36	    performance: { now: () => 1000 },
37	    Image: class { set src(value) { this._src = value; } get src() { return this._src; } },
38	    setTimeout, clearTimeout, Math, JSON, Date,
39	    window: {},
40	  };
41	  context.window = context;
42	  vm.createContext(context);

44	  for (const file of ['i18n.js', 'data.js', 'ferd-skills.js', 'game.js']) {
45	    vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, file), 'utf8'), context, { filename: file });
46	  }
47	  return context;
48	}

Current state: 10 failing tests.
- 8 from faction-traits (the new feature, partially implemented)
- 2 from faction-lock (regressions caused by the mastery gate: `setFaction('echo')`/`setFaction('mooncell')` now rejected pre-mastery, and chips disabled check)

The faction-lock tests conflict with the new mastery gate:
1. "each faction is frozen..." — iterates all 5 keys, calls setFaction(key) expecting true. echo/mooncell now return false pre-mastery. Also asserts all chips disabled — with new locked styling, locked chips remain enabled (per plan: keep enabled so click shows toast). Hmm wait, during a run, ALL chips should be disabled (run-lock). The plan said `c.disabled = locked (run-lock) only`. During run, all disabled including locked-by-mastery ones. So chips.every(disabled) should still hold during run. The failure is probably at `setFaction('echo') === true` pre-mastery.
2. "returning to the menu unlocks selection" — `setFaction('echo')` expects true in menu, but now rejected without mastery.

How to resolve the conflict? The old tests were written before mastery existed. The new feature intentionally changes behavior: D/E require mastery. So the old tests need updating to master a basic faction first (the traits test uses `masterBasic(ctx)` helper = addFactionXp(1e9)).

But wait — "start options can lock a faction without changing the saved menu preference first" expects `startGame({factionKey:'mooncell'})` → S.factionKey === 'mooncell' and it PASSED. That's because startGame coercion isn't implemented yet. Once I implement coercion, this old test will fail too. And the new traits test "startGame coerces a locked faction to an unlocked one" expects the opposite. So the old test must be updated to master first (then mooncell start works).

The right approach: update faction-lock.test.cjs to account for mastery — master a basic faction before testing D/E. That preserves the original intent (run-freezing behavior) while accommodating the new gate. Let me check the transcript to see if the previous session planned this. 103	const FSAVE_KEY = 'aerd_factions_v1';
104	const GRADE_FROM_VAL = ['E','D','C','B','A','S','EX','STAR'];
105	let factionSave = loadFactionSave();
106	function loadFactionSave(){
107	  const base = { cur:'aurel', fac:{} };
108	  FACTIONS.forEach(f=>base.fac[f.key]={lv:1,xp:0});
109	  try{
110	    const raw = localStorage.getItem(FSAVE_KEY);
111	    if (raw){
112	      const p = JSON.parse(raw);
113	      if (p && p.fac){
114	        if (FACTION_BY_KEY[p.cur]) base.cur = p.cur;
115	        FACTIONS.forEach(f=>{
116	          const s = p.fac[f.key];
117	          if (s && typeof s.lv==='number') base.fac[f.key]={ lv:Math.min(FACTION_MAX_LEVEL,Math.max(1,s.lv|0)), xp:Math.max(0,s.xp|0) };
118	        });
119	      }
120	    }
121	  }catch(e){}
122	  return base;
123	}
124	function persistFactionSave(){ try{ localStorage.setItem(FSAVE_KEY, JSON.stringify(factionSave)); }catch(e){} }
125	const curFaction = () => FACTION_BY_KEY[(S.mode !== 'menu' && S.factionKey) || factionSave.cur];
126	const facLevel = () => factionSave.fac[curFaction().key].lv;
127	const facMul = () => 1 + (facLevel()-1)*0.05;
128	const facMastered = () => facLevel() >= FACTION_MAX_LEVEL;
129	const basicMastered = () => FACTIONS.some(f=>!f.reqMaster && factionSave.fac[f.key].lv>=FACTION_MAX_LEVEL);
130	const factionUnlocked = f => !f.reqMaster || basicMastered();
131	function addFactionXp(amount){
132	  const st = factionSave.fac[curFaction().key];
133	  if (st.lv >= FACTION_MAX_LEVEL) return;
134	  st.xp += Math.round(amount);
135	  let leveled = false;
136	  while (st.lv < FACTION_MAX_LEVEL && st.xp >= FACTION_XP_REQ[st.lv-1]){ st.xp -= FACTION_XP_REQ[st.lv-1]; st.lv++; leveled=true; }
137	  if (st.lv >= FACTION_MAX_LEVEL) st.xp = 0;
138	  persistFactionSave();
139	  if (leveled){
140	    AudioFX.ascend();
141	    toast(t('fac.rankUp',{name:curFaction().name,lv:st.lv})+(facMastered()?t('fac.masteredSuffix'):''));
142	    log(t('fac.rankUpLog',{name:curFaction().name,lv:st.lv}), 'epic');
143	  }
144	  syncFaction();
145	}
146	function setFaction(key){
147	  if (!FACTION_BY_KEY[key]) return false;
148	  if (!factionUnlocked(FACTION_BY_KEY[key])){ toast(t('fac.unlockNeed'), true); return false; }
149	  if (S.mode !== 'menu' && S.mode !== 'lobby'){
150	    toast(t('fac.lockedToast'), true);
151	    return false;
152	  }
153	  if (factionSave.cur === key) return true;
154	  factionSave.cur = key; persistFactionSave();
155	  AudioFX.click();
156	  log(t('fac.sworn',{name:curFaction().name,tag:curFaction().tag}), 'good');
157	  toast(`⚑ ${curFaction().name}`);
158	  syncFaction(); syncStats();
159	  return true;
160	}
161	function onKillReward(e, p){
162	  const f = curFaction();
163	  addFactionXp(f.key==='mooncell' ? 150 : 100);
164	  if (f.key==='mooncell'){

179	function factionActive(){
180	  if (S.mode!=='play') return;

183	    if (S.facCd.super>0) return;

439	  else if (f.activeType==='pickup'){ sub=t('fac.pickupSub'); dis=dis||S.wisps<1||!S.bench.includes(null); }

442	  else if (f.key==='echo'){ sub=S.facCd.focus>0?`${Math.ceil(S.facCd.focus)}s`:t('fac.cdSub',{n:f.cd}); dis=dis||S.facCd.focus>0; }
443	  btn.disabled=dis;

445	  $('facActiveDesc').textContent=tl(f,'activeDesc');

500	  const pool = allUnits().filter(x=>!(x.loc===baseRef.loc && x.idx===baseRef.idx));
501	  const used = new Set();

503	    const found = pool.find(x=>x.u.id===mid && !used.has(x.u.uid));

505	    return { id:mid, have:!!found, ref:found };
506	  });
507	}

565	function giveServant(grade, source){
566	  const idx = S.bench.indexOf(null);
567	  if (idx===-1){ toast(t('toast.benchFull'), true); return false; }
568	  const pool = GACHA_POOL[grade];
569	  const id = pick(pool);
570	  const u = UNIT_BY_ID[id];
571	  S.bench[idx] = { uid:uidSeq++, id, cd:0 };
572	  AudioFX.summon(grade);
573	  const epic = grade==='S'||grade==='EX'||grade==='STAR';
574	  log(`${source} [${GRADES[grade].label}] ${u.name}`, epic?'epic':grade==='A'?'gold':'');
575	  if (epic) toast(t('summon.epicToast',{grade:GRADES[grade].label,name:u.name}));
576	  renderBench(idx); syncStats();
577	  if (S.sel) renderSelected();
578	  return true;
579	}
580	function summon(){
581	  if (S.mode!=='play') return;
582	  const cost = summonCost();
583	  if (S.gold < cost) return toast(t('toast.noGold'), true);
584	  if (!S.bench.includes(null)) return toast(t('toast.benchFull'), true);
585	  S.gold -= cost; S.summons++;
586	  let lv = S.gachaLv;
587	  S.pity++;
588	  if (S.pity >= CFG.pityEvery){ lv = Math.min(CFG.gachaMaxLv, lv+1); S.pity = 0; }
589	  const grade = GACHA_GRADE[lv-1];
590	  if (grade==='S') S.pity = 0;
591	  bump($('stGold').parentElement);
592	  giveServant(grade, t('src.summon'));
593	}
594	function upgradeGacha(){
595	  if (S.mode!=='play' || S.gachaLv>=CFG.gachaMaxLv) return;
596	  const c = CFG.gachaUpCost(S.gachaLv);
597	  if (S.spirit < c) return toast(t('toast.noSpirit'), true);
598	  S.spirit -= c; S.gachaLv++;
599	  AudioFX.ascend();
600	  const g = summonGrade();
601	  log(t('upgrade.log',{lv:S.gachaLv,grade:GRADES[g].label}), 'gold');
602	  toast(t('upgrade.toast',{lv:S.gachaLv,grade:GRADES[g].label}));
603	  bump($('stSpirit').parentElement);
604	  syncStats();
605	}
606	/* ---------------- wisp economy ---------------- */
607	function wispToGold(){
608	  if (S.mode!=='play' || S.wisps<1) return;
609	  S.wisps--;
610	  const amt = Math.floor(rand(50+S.wave*2, 100+S.wave*10));
611	  S.gold += amt; S.goldEarned += amt;
612	  AudioFX.sell(); log(t('wisp.goldLog',{amt}), 'gold');
613	  toast(t('wisp.goldToast',{amt})); bump($('stGold').parentElement); syncStats();
614	}

619	  S.spirit += amt;

633:function tryPlace(x, y, playerId=LOCAL_PLAYER){
function tryPlace(x, y, playerId=LOCAL_PLAYER){
  if (!S.sel || S.sel.loc!=='bench') return false;
  const u = S.bench[S.sel.idx];
  if (!u) return false;
  const reason = placementReason(x, y, S.field);
  if (reason){
    toast(t('toast.place.'+reason), true);
    return false;
  }
  const def = UNIT_BY_ID[u.id];
  if (popUsed()+def.pop > S.popCap) { toast(t('toast.popFull',{used:popUsed(),cap:S.popCap}), true); return false; }
  let idx = S.field.findIndex(v=>!v);
  if (idx<0) idx = S.field.length;
  u.x=x; u.y=y; u.owner=playerId;
  S.field[idx] = u; S.bench[S.sel.idx] = null;
  AudioFX.place();
  ringFx(x, y, GRADES[def.grade].color, 40);
  log(t('toast.deployed',{name:def.name}), 'good');
  select({loc:'field', idx});
  syncStats();
  return true;
}

function doServantGamble(key){
  const g=SERVANT_GAMBLES.find(x=>x.key===key);
  if (S.mode!=='play'||!g) return;
  if (!S.bench.includes(null)) return toast(t('toast.benchFull'), true);
  if (S.gold<g.cost) return toast(t('toast.noGold'), true);
  if (S.spirit<g.spirit) return toast(t('toast.noSpirit'), true);
  S.gold-=g.cost; S.spirit-=g.spirit;
  const chance=servantChance(g);
  if (Math.random()*100<chance){
    S.summons++;
    giveServant(pick(g.grades), t(servantSpecial?'src.specialGamble':'src.servantGamble'));
    banner(t('banner.servantWon'));
  } else {
    AudioFX.err();
    log(t('gamble.servantFailLog',{label:g.label,chance}), 'bad');
    toast(t('gamble.servantFail'), true);
  }
  bump($('stGold').parentElement); syncStats(); syncGambling();
}

/* ---------------- logging / toast ---------------- */
function log(msg, cls=''){
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.textContent = msg; if (cls) el.className = cls;
  const box = $('log');
  box.prepend(el);
  while (box.children.length > 30) box.lastChild.remove();
}
let toastT = null;
function toast(msg, err=false){
  const t = $('toast');
  t.textContent = msg; t.className = 'show' + (err?' err':'');
  clearTimeout(toastT);
  toastT = setTimeout(()=>t.className='', 1900);
  if (err) AudioFX.err();
}
function banner(txt){
  const b = $('waveBanner');
  b.textContent = txt; b.classList.remove('show');
  void b.offsetWidth; b.classList.add('show');
}

#### Thinking

15 penalties with real FERD effects. Now I need to design the penalty system for the web game:

**Data (data.js):** `PENALTIES` array of 15 entries `{key, name, nameTh, desc, descTh, icon}` mapped to implementable effects:

1. `enemyBoost` Photon Torrent — enemies +50% speed → spawnEnemy: e.sp *= 1.5
2. `servantCorrupt` Spiritron Pollution — servants attack 50% slower → unitAttack rate: u.cd = 1/def.rate * 2 (attack speed halved). Implement in update loop: `u.cd = 1/def.rate * (pen('servantCorrupt')?2:1)`.
3. `gachaOnlyE` A little makes a lot — wisp draws only E → wispToGacha forces grade E. (FERD: wisp draw rank=1.) Our wispToGacha gives E/D — force E.
4. `powerfulLeader` King's Army — first enemy of each wave HP×20, bounty×10 → spawnEnemy: if first spawn of wave, hp×20, gold×10, mark visual (bigger/crown).
5. `nurGacha` Surprise Attack — gambling restricted → only low-tier gambles allowed: gold gambles cost≤100, servant gambles grades E/D/C only. Implement: filter available gambles / disable higher ones.
6. `johabRandom` RNG Interference — 30% merge result becomes random same-grade → ascend: 30% chance result = random unit of same grade as result.
7. `sellDisabled` Cash on Delivery — cannot sell/release servants → sellSelected blocked with toast.
8. `enemyHeal` Backup Protocol — enemies regen 0.5% maxHP/s → spawnEnemy e.regen += 0.005 (regen is fraction? check ETYPES regen handling).
9. `raid` RAID 1 — 2× enemies, halved HP/bounty → buildWave count×2, spawn hp×0.5 gold×0.5.
10. `servantStun` Aftereffect — killer stunned 1s on kill → need killer identity. Projectiles carry def but not unit ref. Hmm. damageEnemy(e,dmg,opts) — opts could carry src unit. projectileImpact(pr) calls damageEnemy(pr.t, pr.dmg, {...}) — pr has def but not u. I could add `src:u` to projectiles in fire(). Then on kill, if penalty servantStun and pr.src → src.cd = max(src.cd, 1) (stun = can't attack 1s). For chain/beam instant damage, damageEnemy called directly without src — pass u where available. Let me check projectileImpact and beam code. Simpler: add `src:u` in fire() and in chain/beam damageEnemy calls pass {src:u}. Then in damageEnemy kill branch: `if (pen('servantStun') && opts.src) opts.src.cd = Math.max(opts.src.cd, 1);` Wait but opts is the extra object spread into projectile? fire(target, dmg, extra) → projectile {..., dmg, def, ...extra}. projectileImpact reads pr fields and passes opts. Let me read projectileImpact.
11. `lessWisp` We're one short — wave wisps 2→1? FERD 3→2. Our game gives +2/wave (CFG?). Check endWave wisp grant. Reduce by 1.
12. `summonStasis` Imaginary Stasis — periodic stasis traps stop player units → every 20s, random field units stunned 3s (cd += 3). Implement in update: S.stasisT timer; when fires, pick 2 random field units, set cd = max(cd,3), visual ring. 
13. `summonStorm` Cyber Daemon Storm — storm bounces player units → our units are stationary towers; "bounce" = periodically knock a random servant back to bench? That's harsh. Alternative: storm disarms (stun) random servants periodically + visual. FERD knocks units around. For our grid: every 15s a storm strikes a random deployed servant, stunning 2s and pushing it slightly (x/y jitter within valid area)? Moving could invalidate placement. Simpler real effect: storm periodically disables (stun 2.5s) a random servant with visual vortex. Distinct from stasis (which is 2 units every 20s for 3s). Hmm, they'd be near-duplicates. Let me make storm: every 12s, ALL servants within a random storm circle (radius 90) are stunned 2s + particle vortex visual. That's spatially distinct.
14. `afterImpact` Reverse Blast — boss death explodes, stunning all servants 10s → in damageEnemy boss kill branch: if pen, all field units cd = max(cd, 10)? 10s is brutal but FERD-faithful. Maybe 4s for web balance? The user wants faithful ("ทำแบบนี้เปะๆ"). FERD says 10s. Keep 10s but it's optional penalty. Hmm — with 15 penalties selectable and 5+ for XP bonus, stacking afterImpact+stasis+storm could be miserable but that's the point of penalties. Keep faithful: 10s stun all servants on boss death. Actually let me keep it real: cd += ... set cd = Math.max(u.cd, 10).
15. `lifeHalf` Beautiful Painting — starting lives halved → startGame: S.lives = ceil(diff.lives/2).

**XP reward:** 5+ penalties → faction XP +100% (×2). addFactionXp: amount × (S.penalties.length>=5 ? 2 : 1). Also maybe each penalty gives +10%? FERD: minPenalties 5 → +100%. The i18n says 'penalty.reward': 'Reward: XP +100%', 'penalty.rewardNeed': 'Select 5 or more penalties'. And 'penalty.xpBonus': 'XP bonus: +{pct}%'. So pct = count>=5 ? 100 : 0. Keep simple: ×2 when ≥5.

Wait — but addFactionXp is also called in menu (masterBasic tests use addFactionXp(1e9) in menu). S.penalties must exist on the menu state too (freshState includes penalties:[]). The multiplier uses S.penalties — in menu it's [] → ×1. Fine.

**State:** freshState gets `penalties:[]`. But penalties are selected in the START overlay before the run and applied at run start. So there's a menu-level selection: `let selectedPenalties = new Set()` (like selectedDiff). startGame copies to S.penalties. Locked during play (overlay shows read-only view).

Let me check how selectedDiff works and where the start overlay builds. Also end screen: 'end.penalties' key exists → show count. Let me look at gameOver/end screen code and the start overlay HTML.

**UI:** 
- Topbar button `penaltyBtn` (data-i18n ctl.penalty) — opens overlay; during play read-only.
- Start overlay button `startPenaltyBtn` (start.penaltyBtn).
- Overlay `penaltyOverlay` with title, sub, reward line, grid of 15 penalty cards (checkbox style), count '{n} / 15', XP bonus line, Done button.
- i18n keys exist for chrome; penalty names/descs need nameTh/descTh in data.

**Tests (TDD):** tests/penalty.test.cjs:
1. data: 15 penalties, unique keys, name/desc en+th non-empty.
2. togglePenalty in menu adds/removes; locked during play (returns false, no mutation).
3. startGame applies selectedPenalties to S.penalties; lifeHalf halves lives.
4. XP bonus: with 5+ penalties, addFactionXp doubles (compare xp gain).
5. enemyBoost: spawnEnemy speed ×1.5.
6. raid: buildWave count doubles (cap?), hp halved.
7. sellDisabled blocks sellSelected.
8. gachaOnlyE: wispToGacha gives E.
9. johabRandom: ascend with forced Math.random → result random same grade. (Mock Math.random? vm context Math is real Math — can override ctx.Math.random temporarily? Math object is shared host Math! Overriding ctx.Math.random would override host Math.random since context.Math = Math (same reference). Careful: load-game passes `Math` directly. I can temporarily monkey-patch Math.random in test and restore. Acceptable.)
10. lessWisp: endWave grants 1 less.
11. powerfulLeader: first spawn of wave hp×20.
12. servantStun: killer cd set after kill.
13. enemyHeal: regen added.
14. afterImpact: boss death stuns servants.
15. stasis/storm timers exist and fire.

That's a lot. Let me be pragmatic but thorough — test the key mechanics (2,3,4,5,6,7,8,10,11,12,14) and data integrity. Stasis/storm verify via update() ticks.

664	  let idx = S.field.findIndex(v=>!v);
665	  if (idx<0) idx = S.field.length;

667	  S.field[idx] = u; S.bench[S.sel.idx] = null;
668	  AudioFX.place();

670	  log(t('toast.deployed',{name:def.name}), 'good');
671	  select({loc:'field', idx});
672	  syncStats();
673	  return true;

285	function buildGambling(){
286	  const goldGrid=$('goldGambleGrid'); goldGrid.innerHTML='';
287	  GOLD_GAMBLES.forEach(g=>{
288	    const b=document.createElement('button');

291	    b.onclick=()=>doGoldGamble(g.key);
292	    goldGrid.appendChild(b);
293	  });

296	function buildServantGambles(){
297	  const grid=$('servantGambleGrid'); grid.innerHTML='';
298	  SERVANT_GAMBLES.forEach(g=>{
299	    const chance=servantChance(g);
300	    const b=document.createElement('button');
301	    b.className='gambleBtn'; b.id='servant_'+g.key;
302	    b.style.borderColor=GRADES[g.grades[g.grades.length-1]].color;
303	    b.innerHTML=`<b>${t('gamble.servantLabel',{label:g.label})}</b><span>◈${g.cost}${g.spirit?` + ✧${g.spirit}`:''}</span><em>${t('gamble.success',{n:chance})}</em>`;
304	    b.onclick=()=>doServantGamble(g.key);
305	    grid.appendChild(b);
306	  });
307	  $('servantModeNormal').classList.toggle('sel',!servantSpecial);
308	  $('servantModeSpecial').classList.toggle('sel',servantSpecial);
309	}

319	    $('stock_'+g.key).textContent=t('gamble.charges',{n:st.n,max:g.charges})+(st.n<g.charges?t('gamble.next',{s:Math.ceil(g.cd-st.t)}):'');

326	function doGoldGamble(key){
327	  const g=GOLD_GAMBLES.find(x=>x.key===key);

329	  const st=S.gambleStocks[key];
330	  if (st.n<1) return toast(t('gamble.noCharges'), true);

332	  st.n--; S.gold-=g.cost;

337	    log(t('gamble.goldLog',{label:tl(g,'label'),amt,outcome:t(outcome==='lost'?'gamble.outLost':outcome==='same'?'gamble.outSame':'gamble.outWon')}), outcome==='lost'?'bad':'gold');
338	    toast(outcome==='lost'?t('gamble.goldLost',{amt}):outcome==='same'?t('gamble.goldSame'):t('gamble.goldWon',{amt}));
339	    outcome==='lost'?AudioFX.err():AudioFX.sell();

343	    log(t('gamble.spiritLog',{amt}), amt?'good':'bad');
344	    toast(amt?t('gamble.spiritWon',{amt}):t('gamble.spiritNone'));
345	    amt?AudioFX.summon('A'):AudioFX.err();
346	    bump($('stSpirit').parentElement);
347	  }
348	  bump($('stGold').parentElement); syncStats(); syncGambling();
349	}

514:function skillsHTML(u, wrapClass){
function skillsHTML(u, wrapClass){
  const list = u.ferdSkills || [];
  if (!list.length) return '';
  return `<div class="${wrapClass}"><div class="skHead">${t('sel.skill')}</div>` +
    list.map(s=>`<div class="sk"><b class="skn">${esc(s.name)}</b>${s.desc?`<span class="skd">${esc(s.desc).replace(/\n/g,'<br>')}</span>`:''}</div>`).join('') +
    `</div>`;
}
function renderSelected(){
  const none = $('selNone'), card = $('selCard');
  const ref = S.sel && (S.sel.loc==='bench'?S.bench[S.sel.idx]:S.field[S.sel.idx]);
  if (!ref){ none.style.display='block'; card.style.display='none'; return; }
  none.style.display='none'; card.style.display='flex';
  const u = UNIT_BY_ID[ref.id], g = GRADES[u.grade];
  $('selImg').src = portraitSVG(u); $('selImg').style.borderColor = g.color;
  $('selName').textContent = u.name;
  $('selMeta').innerHTML =
    `<span class="gradeChip" style="background:${g.color}">${u.grade}</span><span>${u.cls}</span><span>⛨${u.pop}</span>` +
    (S.sel.loc==='field'?`<span style="color:var(--success)">${t('sel.onField')}</span>`:`<span style="color:var(--info)">${t('sel.onBench')}</span>`);
  $('ssDmg').textContent = u.dmg; $('ssRate').textContent = u.rate; $('ssRange').textContent = u.range;
  $('selDesc').textContent = unitDesc(u);
  $('selSkills').innerHTML = skillsHTML(u,'selSkills');
  $('sellBtn').textContent = t('sel.release',{n:Math.max(1,Math.round(gradeVal(u.grade)/2))});
  $('ascendBtn').style.display='none';
  const rbox = $('recipeBox');
  const recs = recipesFor(ref.id);
  if (!recs.length){ rbox.style.display='none'; return; }

function buildCodexFilters(){
  const fill=(id,values,labelFn,cur)=>{
    const el=$(id); el.innerHTML='';
    const all=document.createElement('option'); all.value=''; all.textContent=t('codex.all'); el.appendChild(all);
    values.forEach(v=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=v;o.textContent=labelFn(v);el.appendChild(o);});
    el.value=cur;
  };
  fill('codexClass',CODEX_CLASSES,v=>v,codexFilter.cls);
  fill('codexGrade',CODEX_GRADES,v=>v,codexFilter.grade);
  fill('codexKind',CODEX_KINDS,v=>t('klabel.'+v),codexFilter.kind);
}
function setCodexFilter(patch){
  Object.assign(codexFilter,patch);
  if('q' in patch)$('codexSearch').value=codexFilter.q;
  if('cls' in patch)$('codexClass').value=codexFilter.cls;
  if('grade' in patch)$('codexGrade').value=codexFilter.grade;
  if('kind' in patch)$('codexKind').value=codexFilter.kind;
  buildCodex(codexTab);
}

Let me write the glossary into the prompts. The agents are general-purpose (can read/write files). I'll run them in background and continue the penalty system meanwhile.

Glossary (consistent across all 4):
- Servant → ผู้รับใช้
- "This Servant's" → "ของผู้รับใช้นี้" / "ผู้รับใช้นี้"
- Passive Art → ความสามารถติดตัว
- Extra Arts → ความสามารถเสริม
- Noble Phantasm → โฮวจน
- damage → ดาเมจ
- attack → การโจมตี / basic attack → การโจมตีปกติ
- attack speed → ความเร็วโจมตี
- range → ระยะโจมตี
- stun → สตั้น
- slow → สโลว์
- splash damage → ดาเมจกระจาย
- shield → โล่
- armor → เกราะ
- gold → ทอง
- MP → MP, HP → HP
- cooldown → คูลดาวน์
- critical → คริติคอล
- chance → โอกาส
- per second → ต่อวินาที
- enemy/enemies → ศัตรู
- kill → ฆ่า
- boss → บอส

13	  fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'ferd-skills-th.js'), 'utf8') +
14	  ';globalThis.FS=FERD_SKILLS;globalThis.NT=FERD_NAME_TH;globalThis.DT=FERD_DESC_TH;', ref);
15	const FS = ref.FS, NT = ref.NT, DT = ref.DT;
16	const all = Object.values(FS).flat();
17	const isFormula = d => d && d.includes('=') && /^\[[A-Z★]+\]/.test(d.trim());
18	
19	test('every FERD skill name has a Thai translation', () => {
20	  const missing = [...new Set(all.map(s => s.name))].filter(n => !NT[n] || !/[\u0E01-\u0E5B]/.test(NT[n]));
21	  assert.deepStrictEqual(missing, [], 'names missing Thai: ' + missing.slice(0, 5).join(' | '));
22	});
23	
24	test('every prose skill description has a Thai translation', () => {
25	  const missing = [...new Set(all.map(s => s.desc).filter(d => d && !isFormula(d)))].filter(d => !DT[d] || !/[\u0E01-\u0E5B]/.test(DT[d]));
26	  assert.deepStrictEqual(missing.length, 0, 'descs missing Thai: ' + missing.slice(0, 2).map(d => d.slice(0, 60)).join(' | '));
27	});
28	
29	test('Thai translations preserve structure markers and numbers', () => {
30	  for (const [en, th] of Object.entries(DT)) {
31	    const enNums = (en.match(/\d+(\.\d+)?%?/g) || []).sort();
32	    const thNums = (th.match(/\d+(\.\d+)?%?/g) || []).sort();
33	    assert.deepStrictEqual(thNums, enNums, 'numbers changed in: ' + en.slice(0, 50));
34	    assert.strictEqual((th.match(/\n/g) || []).length, (en.match(/\n/g) || []).length, 'line breaks changed in: ' + en.slice(0, 50));
35	  }
36	});
37	
38	test('selected card renders Thai skill text in Thai mode and English in English mode', () => {
39	  const ctx = loadGame();
40	  ctx.setLang('th');
41	  ctx.startGame();
42	  ctx.giveServant('A', 'test');
43	  const idx = ctx.Game.S.bench.findIndex(Boolean);
44	  ctx.select({ loc: 'bench', idx });
45	  const u = ctx.Game.S.bench[idx];
46	  const skills = ref.FS[u.id] || [];
47	  const html = ctx.elements.get('selSkills').innerHTML;
48	  if (skills.length) {
49	    const first = skills[0];
50	    assert.ok(html.includes(NT[first.name] || first.name), 'Thai name rendered');
51	    if (first.desc && !isFormula(first.desc)) assert.ok(html.includes((DT[first.desc] || first.desc).split('\n')[0]), 'Thai desc rendered');
52	  }
53	  ctx.setLang('en');
54	  ctx.select({ loc: 'bench', idx });
55	  const enHtml = ctx.elements.get('selSkills').innerHTML;
56	  if (skills.length) assert.ok(enHtml.includes(skills[0].name), 'English name restored');
57	});
58	
59	test('merge formula descriptions render unchanged in Thai mode', () => {
60	  const ctx = loadGame();
61	  ctx.setLang('th');
62	  const formula = all.find(s => isFormula(s.desc));
63	  assert.ok(formula, 'a merge formula exists');
64	  assert.strictEqual(ctx.skillDesc(formula), formula.desc);
65	});
66	
67	test('codex search matches Thai skill text', () => {
68	  const ctx = loadGame();
69	  ctx.setLang('th');
70	  const withThai = Object.entries(ref.FS).find(([id, list]) => list.some(s => s.desc && !isFormula(s.desc) && DT[s.desc]));
71	  assert.ok(withThai, 'a translated unit exists');
72	  const [id, list] = withThai;
73	  const s = list.find(x => x.desc && !isFormula(x.desc) && DT[x.desc]);
74	  const word = DT[s.desc].replace(/[\n\d%.\[\]]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).find(w => w.length >= 4 && /[\u0E01-\u0E5B]/.test(w));
75	  assert.ok(word, 'a Thai search word exists');
76	  const unit = vm.runInContext('UNIT_BY_ID[' + JSON.stringify(id) + ']', ctx);
77	  const text = ctx.unitSearchText(unit);
78	  assert.ok(text.includes(word.toLowerCase()), 'haystack contains Thai word');
79	});

#### Thinking

The test requires that all skill names (including merge formulas like "[A] Andersen Merge" and Korean names) have Thai translations containing Thai characters. In other words, all 329 names need Thai translations — including merge formula names (only the formula's *desc* is kept as-is, the name is translated). Also, Korean skill names need Thai translations too.

Let's collect the exact unique name list and description list from FERD_SKILLS, and generate the translation files. First, let's dump the unique names and descriptions to check the actual set (the tr-*.json batches were created previously, but let's verify they match the actual data).

#### Tool: Bash

**Input:**

```json
{
  "command": "node -e \"\nconst fs=require('fs'),vm=require('vm');const ctx={};vm.createContext(ctx);\nvm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync('anime-extra-random-defense-3d/ferd-skills.js','utf8')+';globalThis.FS=FERD_SKILLS;',ctx);\nconst all=Object.values(ctx.FS).flat();\nconst names=[...new Set(all.map(s=>s.name))].sort();\nconst isFormula=d=>d&&d.includes('=')&&/^\\[[A-Z★]+\\]/.test(d.trim());\nconst descs=[...new Set(all.map(s=>s.desc).filter(d=>d&&!isFormula(d)))].sort();\nconsole.log('names:',names.length,'descs:',descs.length);\nfs.writeFileSync('_ferd/out/all-names.json',JSON.stringify(names,null,1));\nfs.writeFileSync('_ferd/out/all-descs.json',JSON.stringify(descs,null,1));\n\"",
  "description": "Extract unique skill names and prose descs",
  "dir_path": "E:\\048e0eb0\\anime-extra-random-defense"
}
```

**Response:**

names: 329 descs: 252

329 names, 252 prose explanations. The previous batch files were tr-names-a (165 items, ending with "[A] Andersen Merge") and tr-names-b (164 items). But the sort order might be different. Let me verify that the batches cover exactly the same set. Actually, there's an easier way: read all-names.json and all-descs.json directly and translate them myself. That's 329+252 = 581 strings. Big, but doable in several Write calls.

#### Thinking

Alright, now I've got the full list of names. Rather than hand-writing 329 entries (including repetitive merge names), I can generate the merge names programmatically: the pattern `[X] NAME Merge` → `[X] NAME ฟิวชัน`. But the test requires Thai characters in each translation — "ฟิวชัน" contains Thai characters, so that works. I can build the map in JS: write a generator that maps merge names via regex, and manually translate the 165 non-merge names + Korean names + [Disassembly]/[Transformation]/『Ascension』/[★].

Actually, the simplest and most robust approach is: write ferd-skills-th.js with the following content:
1. Manually translated FERD_NAME_TH entries for the ~165 prose names + special entries.
2. Merge names generated programmatically at load time: iterate over FERD_SKILLS, and for names matching /^\[([A-Z★]+)\] (.+) Merge$/, add `[grade] name ฟิวชัน`. But ferd-skills-th.js is loaded after ferd-skills.js, so FERD_SKILLS is available. This keeps the file small and guarantees exact key coverage. But is it okay for the file to depend on FERD_SKILLS? The test loads ferd-skills.js first, then loads ferd-skills-th.js — no problem. index.html also loads them in order — no problem.

But wait — proper nouns: the user wanted original names for units, but skill names can keep proper nouns. The previous agent's instructions said to keep proper nouns. For merge names, keep the character name (e.g., "[A] Andersen ฟิวชัน"). Hmm, but unit names in the game are original names… FERD skill names still reference the original Fate names (the user said to keep character models/appearance but use original names for units; skill names are kept as FERD data). The screenshot showed "Elisabeth Bathory Altar Merge" in the skills. So keeping proper nouns in the skill translation is consistent with the earlier decision.

For descs, I need to read all-descs.json and translate the 252 prose descs while preserving numbers and line breaks. Let me read it.

#### Thinking

I've grasped the overall scope. 329 names, 252 prose descriptions. I'll translate these myself. Strategy:

1. **Names**: Write FERD_NAME_TH with manual translations for the ~165 non-merge names + special entries. For merge names (pattern `[X] NAME Merge`), generate them programmatically inside ferd-skills-th.js by iterating over FERD_SKILLS — this guarantees exact key matches and avoids typos. Korean names get Thai translations.

2. **Descriptions**: Write FERD_DESC_TH with manual translations for all 252 entries. This is the bulk of the work. Numbers and line breaks must be preserved exactly. Many descriptions share the same flavor text with different numbers — I can build them via template functions to reduce error risk. Actually, a safer approach: write a generator script that has translation templates keyed by the "title" portion (first line) and generates translations for variants. But each variant has different numbers embedded in the mechanics text, and the test requires exact number preservation. Writing a template system with placeholders risks number mismatches.

Alternative practical approach: translate each description fully by hand into a JS object. 252 entries × ~400 chars = ~100KB of Thai text. Large, but doable in 3–4 Write/Edit chunks. To reduce number errors, I can write a validation step after writing: the test itself checks numbers/line breaks. If mismatches come up, fix them.

Actually, a smarter approach for reliability: build the translations programmatically from templates. Each description has the structure `Title\n\nFlavor\n\nMechanics`. The flavor text is repeated per title. The mechanics lines contain numbers. I can write a Thai mechanics translation per unique mechanics block. But mechanics blocks also differ subtly in wording.

Given the constraints, the most reliable path is: write the full FERD_DESC_TH object by hand, carefully preserving numbers and \n counts. Write it as a JS file with entries. The test will catch any number/line-break errors, which I'll then fix. Let's proceed systematically.

To manage size, I'll write ferd-skills-th.js in parts:
- Part 1: header + FERD_NAME_TH (manual names + generated merges)
- Part 2 onward: FERD_DESC_TH entries (252 of them)

144	  }

146	}

734	function buildWave(w){
735	  const q = [];
736	  const count = Math.min(30, 8 + Math.floor(w*0.55));
737	  const isBoss = w%5===0;
738	  for (let i=0;i<count;i++){
739	    let type='normal';
740	    const r=Math.random();
741	    if (w>=20 && r<0.16) type='regen';
742	    else if (w>=10 && r<0.36) type='armor';
743	    else if (w>=4 && r<0.60) type='swift';
744	    q.push(type);
745	  }
746	  if (isBoss) q.push(w%10===0?'bigboss':'boss');
747	  return q;
748	}
749	const ETYPES = {
750	  normal:{ hp:1,   sp:74,  r:9,  color:'#ff7a4d', gold:1,   leak:1 },
751	  swift: { hp:0.6, sp:120, r:7,  color:'#3de0c8', gold:1,   leak:1 },
752	  armor: { hp:1.6, sp:58,  r:10, color:'#9ab0c8', gold:1.4, leak:1, armor:0.3 },
753	  regen: { hp:1.2, sp:66,  r:9,  color:'#5dc86a', gold:1.3, leak:1, regen:0.02 },
754	  boss:  { hp:8,  sp:46,  r:17, color:'#e14dff', gold:12,  leak:5, boss:true },
755	  bigboss:{hp:16, sp:40,  r:21, color:'#ff3d5e', gold:25,  leak:8, boss:true },
756	};
757	function startWave(){
758	  if (S.mode!=='play' || S.waveActive) return;
759	  S.wave++;
760	  S.waveActive = true;
761	  S.spawnQueue = buildWave(S.wave);
762	  S.spawnT = 0.4;
763	  const boss = S.wave%5===0;
764	  banner(boss ? t('banner.waveBoss',{n:S.wave}) : t('banner.wave',{n:S.wave}));
765	  boss ? AudioFX.boss() : AudioFX.wave();
766	  log(boss?t('log.boss',{n:S.wave}):t('log.wave',{n:S.wave}), boss?'bad':'');
767	  syncStats();
768	}
769	function spawnEnemy(type){
770	  const t = ETYPES[type], w = S.wave;
771	  const finalBoss = t.boss && w===S.diff.waves;
772	  const hp = Math.round((10 + 4*w + 0.5*w*w) * t.hp * S.diff.hp * (finalBoss?1.5:1));
773	  S.enemies.push({
774	    uid:uidSeq++, type, d:0, hp, maxHp:hp, sp:t.sp*S.diff.speed*rand(0.92,1.08), r:t.r, color:t.color,
775	    gold:Math.round((3+w*0.7)*t.gold*S.diff.gold), leak:t.leak, boss:!!t.boss,
776	    armor:t.armor||0, regen:t.regen||0, slowT:0, slowF:0, stunT:0, dots:[], dead:false,
777	  });
778	}
779	function endWave(){
780	  S.waveActive = false;
781	  const interest = Math.min(60, Math.floor(S.gold*0.08));
782	  const bonus = Math.round((30 + S.wave*5) * S.diff.gold) + interest;
783	  S.gold += bonus; S.goldEarned += bonus;
784	  S.wisps += CFG.wispsPerWave;
785	  const spiritReward = curFaction().key==='kitsune' ? 2 : 1;

788	  log(t('log.waveCleared',{n:S.wave,bonus,interest:interest?t('log.interest',{n:interest}):'',wisps:CFG.wispsPerWave,spirit:spiritReward}), 'gold');
789	  bump($('stGold').parentElement);

820	  if (e.dead) return;

824	  if (opts.slow){ e.slowT = Math.max(e.slowT, opts.slowT||1.2); e.slowF = Math.max(e.slowF, opts.slow); }
825	  if (opts.stun) e.stunT = Math.max(e.stunT, opts.stun);
826	  if (opts.greed){ S.gold += opts.greed; S.goldEarned += opts.greed; }
827	  const p = pathPos(e.d);

829	  if (e.hp <= 0){
830	    e.dead = true;
831	    S.kills++; S.gold += e.gold; S.goldEarned += e.gold;
832	    onKillReward(e, p);
833	    burst(p.x, p.y, e.color, e.boss?30:8);
834	    if (e.boss){ S.shake = Math.max(S.shake, 9); ringFx(p.x,p.y,e.color,80); log(t('log.bossDestroyed',{gold:e.gold}), 'good'); }
835	    AudioFX.kill();

840	function enemiesInRange(x,y,r){
841	  const out=[];

845	    if ((p.x-x)**2+(p.y-y)**2 <= r*r) out.push({e,p});
846	  }
847	  return out;
848	}
849	function unitAttack(u, def, x, y){
850	  const inR = enemiesInRange(x,y,def.range);

856	  AudioFX.shoot();
857	  const fire = (target, dmg, extra={}) => {
858	    S.projectiles.push({ uid:uidSeq++, x, y, t:target.e, tx:target.p.x, ty:target.p.y, sp:460*(curFaction().key==='aurel'?1.25:1)*(u.crest?1.5:1), dmg, color:g.color, def, ...extra });
859	  };

947	/* ---------------- update (driven by the 3D loop) ---------------- */
948	function update(dt){
949	  S.time += dt;
950	  if (S.facCd.seal>0) S.facCd.seal=Math.max(0,S.facCd.seal-dt);
951	  if (S.facCd.focus>0) S.facCd.focus=Math.max(0,S.facCd.focus-dt);
952	  if (S.facCd.super>0) S.facCd.super=Math.max(0,S.facCd.super-dt);
953	  for (const g of GOLD_GAMBLES){
954	    const st=S.gambleStocks[g.key];
955	    if (st.n<g.charges){
956	      st.t+=dt;
957	      if (st.t>=g.cd){ st.t-=g.cd; st.n++; }
958	    } else st.t=0;
959	  }
960	  if (S.focusT>0) S.focusT=Math.max(0,S.focusT-dt);
961	  S.facUiT=(S.facUiT||0)+dt;
962	  if (S.facUiT>0.25){ S.facUiT=0; syncFaction(); syncGambling(); }
963	  if (S.waveActive && S.spawnQueue.length){
964	    S.spawnT -= dt;
965	    if (S.spawnT <= 0){
966	      const type = S.spawnQueue.shift();
967	      spawnEnemy(type);
968	      S.spawnT = type==='boss' ? 1.6 : Math.max(0.35, 0.95 - S.wave*0.02);
969	    }
970	  }
971	  if (S.auto && !S.waveActive && S.mode==='play' && S.wave>0 && S.wave<S.diff.waves){
972	    S.autoT -= dt;
973	    if (S.autoT<=0) startWave();
974	  }
975	  for (const e of S.enemies){
976	    if (e.dead) continue;
977	    if (e.slowT>0) e.slowT-=dt;
978	    if (e.stunT>0) e.stunT-=dt;
979	    for (const d of e.dots){ d.t-=dt; if (d.t>0){ e.hp -= d.dps*dt*(1-e.armor); } }
980	    e.dots = e.dots.filter(d=>d.t>0);
981	    if (e.regen) e.hp = Math.min(e.maxHp, e.hp + e.maxHp*e.regen*dt);
982	    if (e.hp<=0){
983	      e.dead=true; S.kills++; S.gold+=e.gold; S.goldEarned+=e.gold;
984	      const p=pathPos(e.d); onKillReward(e,p); burst(p.x,p.y,'#5dc86a',6); AudioFX.kill(); syncStats();
985	      continue;
986	    }
987	    const slow = e.slowT>0 ? 1-e.slowF : 1;
988	    e.d += e.sp * slow * (e.stunT>0?0:1) * dt;
989	    if (e.d >= PATH_LEN){
990	      e.dead = true;
991	      S.lives -= e.leak;
992	      S.leakFlash = 0.5; S.shake = Math.max(S.shake, e.boss?10:5);
993	      AudioFX.leak();
994	      log(t('log.leaked',{type:e.boss?t('enemy.boss'):t('enemy.normal'),n:e.leak}), 'bad');
995	      bump($('stLives').parentElement);
996	      syncStats();
997	      if (S.lives<=0){ S.lives=0; syncStats(); return gameOver(false); }
998	    }
999	  }
1000	  S.enemies = S.enemies.filter(e=>!e.dead);
1001	  if (S.waveActive && !S.spawnQueue.length && !S.enemies.length) endWave();
1002	  S.field.forEach((u,i)=>{
1003	    if (!u) return;
1004	    u.cd -= dt;
1005	    if (u.cd<=0){
1006	      const def = UNIT_BY_ID[u.id];
1007	      if (unitAttack(u, def, u.x, u.y)) u.cd = 1/def.rate;
1008	      else u.cd = 0.08;
1009	    }
1010	  });
1011	  for (const pr of S.projectiles){
1012	    if (pr.t && !pr.t.dead){ const p=pathPos(pr.t.d); pr.tx=p.x; pr.ty=p.y; }
1013	    const dx=pr.tx-pr.x, dy=pr.ty-pr.y, dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
1014	    const step=(pr.fast?640:pr.sp)*dt;
1015	    if (dist<=step+4){ pr.done=true; projectileImpact(pr); }
1016	    else { pr.x+=dx/dist*step; pr.y+=dy/dist*step; }
1017	  }
1018	  S.projectiles = S.projectiles.filter(p=>!p.done);
1019	  for (const b of S.beams) b.t+=dt;
1020	  S.beams = S.beams.filter(b=>b.t<b.life);
1021	  for (const r of S.rings) r.t+=dt;
1022	  S.rings = S.rings.filter(r=>r.t<r.life);
1023	  for (const p of S.particles){ p.t+=dt; p.x+=p.vx*dt; p.y+=p.vy*dt; p.vx*=0.96; p.vy*=0.96; }
1024	  S.particles = S.particles.filter(p=>p.t<p.life);
1025	  for (const f of S.floats){ f.t+=dt; f.y-=28*dt; }
1026	  S.floats = S.floats.filter(f=>f.t<f.life);
1027	  S.shake = Math.max(0, S.shake - dt*22);
1028	  S.leakFlash = Math.max(0, S.leakFlash - dt);

#### Thinking

All the necessary information is gathered. Now let's write the RED test for the penalty system, then implement it. Design:

**Data (data.js)**: Insert `PENALTIES` array before PATH. 15 entries with key, icon, name, nameTh, desc, descTh. Keys:
1. enemyBoost — Photon Torrent — Enemies 50% faster
2. servantCorrupt — Spiritron Pollution — Servants 50% slower (attack rate halved)
3. gachaOnlyE — A Little Makes a Lot — Wisp gacha E only
4. powerfulLeader — King's Army — First enemy of each wave HP×20, bounty×10
5. nurfGacha — Surprise Attack — Gambling restricted (only g10/g100 gold + E–D servant gamble)
6. johabRandom — RNG Interference — 30% chance ascension result becomes a random unit of the same grade
7. sellDisabled — Cash on Delivery — Cannot sell servants
8. enemyHeal — Backup Protocol — All enemies regen 0.5% max HP/s
9. raid — RAID 1 — 2× enemies, HP/bounty halved
10. servantStun — Aftereffect — Servant that lands a killing blow is stunned for 1s
11. lessWisp — We're One Short — Wisps per wave −1
12. summonStasis — Imaginary Stasis — Every 20s, stun 2 random field units for 3s (FERD: stasis field stops units; simplify: stun 2 random field units for 3s)
13. summonStorm — Cyber Daemon Storm — Every 15s, storm stuns all field units in a random area for 2s (simplify: stun random 3 units for 2s + shake)
14. afterImpact — Reverse Blast — Boss death stuns all servants for 5s (FERD is 10s; use 5s for playability? FERD is 10s stun + knockback. Our servants don't move, so stun 10s is harsh but faithful. Use 5s as a web adaptation… the penalties.json description says 10s. Keep 10s? That's brutal. FERD faithfulness matters to the user. FERD: all player servants stunned for 10s and knocked back. Implement 10s stun? Hmm, knockback could displace units — too complex. Stun 10s is faithful enough. Actually, let's use 5s for playability — no, the user wants FERD faithfulness. The penalties.json I extracted says "all player servants stunned for 10s". Implement 10s.)
15. lifeHalf — Beautiful Painting — Lives halved

XP reward: 5 or more → faction XP ×2 (i.e. +100%).

**State**:
- `let selectedPenalties = new Set();` at menu level (near selectedDiff)
- freshState: `penalties:[], penaltyT:0, leaderSpawned:false` — actually leaderSpawned resets per wave; track via S.waveSpawned counter or a flag reset in startWave. Add `waveSpawnCount:0` reset in startWave; powerfulLeader applies when waveSpawnCount===0.
- startGame: `S.penalties=[...selectedPenalties]` (validate keys), lifeHalf → S.lives=Math.max(1,Math.floor(S.lives/2)).
- Helper `const hasPen = k => S.penalties.includes(k);`
- addFactionXp: `if (S.mode!=='menu' && S.penalties.length>=5) amount*=2;` — note: addFactionXp is also called from menu context (tests). Apply multiplier only when penalties are active (S.penalties.length>=5 implies in-run since freshState is empty). Simply: `amount *= S.penalties.length>=5 ? 2 : 1`.

**Mechanic hooks**:
1. enemyBoost: spawnEnemy `sp:t.sp*S.diff.speed*rand(...)*(hasPen('enemyBoost')?1.5:1)`
2. servantCorrupt: field attack cd `u.cd = (1/def.rate)*(hasPen('servantCorrupt')?2:1)`
3. gachaOnlyE: wispToGacha grade → `hasPen('gachaOnlyE') ? 'E' : (S.wave<15?'E':...)`
4. powerfulLeader: spawnEnemy — when S.waveSpawnCount===0 (increment after): hp×20, gold×10, mark e.leader=true. Reset S.waveSpawnCount=0 in startWave.
5. nurfGacha: syncGambling disables restricted ones; doGoldGamble only allows g10/g100; doServantGamble only ED. Add `gambleAllowed(g)` helper: if hasPen('nurfGacha'): gold → g.key==='g10'||g.key==='g100'; servant → g.key==='ED'. Check in do functions + disabled state in sync.
6. johabRandom: ascend — after `base.id = r.result`, if hasPen && Math.random()<0.3 → base.id = random unit of the same grade as the result. FERD: "replace result with a random servant of the same grade". Grade of result. Pick from UNITS of that grade. Log/toast.
7. sellDisabled: sellSelected → `if (hasPen('sellDisabled')) return toast(t('penalty.sellBlocked'), true);` need i18n key. Also disable sellBtn in renderSelected? Simplest: block in sellSelected + sync UI disable. Add key penalty.sellBlocked EN/TH.
8. enemyHeal: spawnEnemy `regen:(t.regen||0)+(hasPen('enemyHeal')?0.005:0)`
9. raid: buildWave count×2 (cap 60); spawnEnemy hp×0.5, gold×0.5.
10. servantStun: need source unit on kill. Projectiles: add `src:u.uid` to fire(); hitOne passes opts.src=pr.src; chain/beam damageEnemy calls pass {src:u.uid}; damageEnemy kill branch: `if (opts.src && hasPen('servantStun')){ const f=unitByUid(opts.src); if(f&&f.loc==='field') f.u.stunT=1; }` — field units need a stunT property; update loop: `if(u.stunT>0){u.stunT-=dt;return;}` before cd logic. Also DoT kills (update loop) have no src — that's fine, FERD triggers on kill by unit; DoT kill attribution is ambiguous; skip.
   Also kill via damageEnemy from kitsune nuke etc. has no src — fine.
11. lessWisp: endWave `S.wisps += Math.max(0, CFG.wispsPerWave - (hasPen('lessWisp')?1:0))` — log uses wisps variable; adjust.
12. summonStasis: update — `if(hasPen('summonStasis')){ S.stasisT=(S.stasisT||0)+dt; if(S.stasisT>=20){S.stasisT=0; stun 2 random field units for 3s; toast/log} }` — FERD: first after 10s, then every 20s. Initialize S.stasisT=10 in startGame when penalty active (i.e. countdown from 10 → fires when reaches 20? Simpler: S.penT timer). Add generic timers to freshState: `penStasis:10, penStorm:3`. Hmm, keep it simple: freshState `stasisT:0, stormT:0`; startGame sets stasisT=10 (so first fires 10s later: fires when >=20). Storm: FERD creates typhoon 3s after init, persists and bounces units periodically. Web adaptation: every 15s storm stuns random 3 field units for 2s + shake. stormT starts at 12 (first after 3s).
13. summonStorm: as above.
14. afterImpact: damageEnemy kill branch `if (e.boss && hasPen('afterImpact')){ S.field.forEach(u=>{if(u)u.stunT=Math.max(u.stunT||0,10);}); log/toast; shake }`
15. lifeHalf: startGame.

**UI**:
- Top bar: add penalty button `id="penaltyBtn"` with data-i18n ctl.penalty, title ctl.penaltyTitle.
- Start overlay: add button `id="startPenaltyBtn"` data-i18n start.penaltyBtn before/after roomControls.
- Overlay: `id="penaltyOverlay"` with box: head (title + close), sub, reward note, grid `id="penaltyGrid"`, footer count + done button.
- buildPenalties(): render 15 cards; each card toggles selection when S.mode==='menu' (or lobby); when in play, locked (show selected state, click → toast penalty.locked). Card shows icon, name (tl), desc (tl), check state.
- syncPenaltyUI(): update count text, reward highlight (5+ → reward active), button badge count.
- Top bar penaltyBtn opens overlay anytime (view mode when locked). startPenaltyBtn opens overlay in menu.
- endStats: add penalties count row when >0: `[S.penalties.length, t('end.penalties')]`.
- startGame: log applied penalties; banner? Log each penalty name. Add i18n key penalty.appliedLog: '☣ Penalty active: {name}' / Thai. And penalty.sellBlocked, penalty.lockedToast (reuse penalty.locked).
- onLangChange: rebuild penalty grid if overlay open + sync.

**Tests (RED)**: tests/penalty.test.cjs:
1. PENALTIES has 15 entries with key/name/nameTh/desc/descTh.
2. selectedPenalties toggling + startGame copies to S.penalties; lifeHalf halves lives.
3. XP multiplier: 5 penalties → addFactionXp(100) adds 200.
4. enemyBoost: spawnEnemy speed ×1.5 (compare with/without — deterministic? rand(0.92,1.08) varies; check ratio bounds, or stub Math.random). Use vm Math.random stub = 0.5 → rand returns midpoint. Actually rand(a,b) implementation — check. Let's just assert e.sp equals base*1.5 within rand range: compute expected range.
5. gachaOnlyE: with penalty, wispToGacha at wave 20 always gives E (stub Math.random to return 0.9 → would normally give D).
6. sellDisabled blocks sell.
7. raid doubles buildWave length.
8. powerfulLeader: first spawn has hp×20 & gold×10.
9. enemyHeal adds regen 0.005.
10. lessWisp reduces endWave wisps by 1.
11. johabRandom: stub Math.random<0.3 → ascend result is random same-grade (not r.result).
12. servantStun: killer unit gets stunT after projectile kill.
13. afterImpact: boss death stuns field units.
14. Penalty locked during play: togglePenalty returns false / no change.
15. UI: penaltyOverlay exists, grid has 15 cards, top bar button exists.

Let me check load-game.cjs to see how the harness works and what's exposed (ctx.Game, ctx.elements, etc.), and how Math.random can be stubbed.

I have the full picture now. Writing the RED tests for the penalty system first.

14	    appendChild(child) { this.children.push(child); child.parentElement = this; return child; },
15	    prepend(child) { this.children.unshift(child); child.parentElement = this; return child; },
16	    remove() { if (this.parentElement && this.parentElement.children) this.parentElement.children = this.parentElement.children.filter(x => x !== this); },
17	    get lastChild() { return this.children[this.children.length - 1] || null; },
18	    addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {}, setAttribute() {},
19	  };
20	  el.parentElement = { classList: { add() {}, remove() {}, toggle() {} } };
21	  return el;
22	}
23	
24	function loadGame() {
25	  const elements = new Map();
26	  const document = {
27	    getElementById(id) { if (!elements.has(id)) elements.set(id, element(id)); return elements.get(id); },
28	    createElement(tag) { return element(tag); },
29	    querySelectorAll() { return []; },
30	    addEventListener() {},
31	  };
32	  const storage = new Map();
33	  const context = {
34	    console, document, elements,
35	    localStorage: { getItem: k => storage.get(k) || null, setItem: (k, v) => storage.set(k, v) },
36	    performance: { now: () => 1000 },
37	    Image: class { set src(value) { this._src = value; } get src() { return this._src; } },
38	    setTimeout, clearTimeout, Math, JSON, Date,
39	    window: {},
40	  };
41	  context.window = context;
42	  vm.createContext(context);
43	  const root = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', 'anime-extra-random-defense-3d');
44	  for (const file of ['i18n.js', 'data.js', 'ferd-skills.js', 'ferd-skills-th.js', 'game.js']) {
45	    vm.runInContext(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, file), 'utf8'), context, { filename: file });
46	  }
47	  return context;
48	}
49	
50	function unit(id = 'ATE', uid = 1) {
51	  return { id, uid, cd: 0 };
52	}
53	
54	module.exports = { loadGame, unit };

The harness shares the host's `Math` object — so if I stub Math.random on the host side, it affects the context too (since it's the same object reference). I can monkey-patch Math.random in tests and restore it later.

Note: `classList.contains()` always returns false — so the overlay's "show" check doesn't work in tests, but className assignment does work. For the penalty overlay, use className assignment where tests need it.

Tests can access function declarations directly on ctx (function declarations become globals in vm). `togglePenalty` as a function declaration → ctx.togglePenalty works. `selectedPenalties` as a let → doesn't become a global; access via vm.runInContext. PENALTIES const in data.js → access via vm.runInContext('PENALTIES', ctx).

---
function matStatus(recipe, baseRef){
  const pool = allUnits().filter(x=>!(x.loc===baseRef.loc && x.idx===baseRef.idx));
  const used = new Set();
  return recipe.mats.map(mid=>{
    const found = pool.find(x=>x.u.id===mid && !used.has(x.u.uid));
    if (found) used.add(found.u.uid);
    return { id:mid, have:!!found, ref:found };
  });
}

```js
  S.field.forEach((u,i)=>{
    if (!u) return;
```

New version:
```js
  S.field.forEach((u,i)=>{
    if (!u) return;
    if (u.stunT>0){ u.stunT=Math.max(0,u.stunT-dt); return; }
    u.cd -= dt;
    if (u.cd<=0){
      const def = UNIT_BY_ID[u.id];
      if (unitAttack(u, def, u.x, u.y)) u.cd = (1/def.rate)*(hasPen('servantCorrupt')?2:1);
      else u.cd = 0.08;
    }
  });
```

And the periodic penalties — insert after the focusT line, or near facUiT:
```js
  if (S.mode==='play'){
    if (hasPen('summonStasis')){
      S.stasisT += dt;
      if (S.stasisT>=20){
        S.stasisT = 0;
        const targets = S.field.filter(Boolean).sort(()=>Math.random()-0.5).slice(0,2);
        targets.forEach(u=>{ u.stunT=Math.max(u.stunT||0,3); ringFx(u.x,u.y,'#8f7bff',50); });
        if (targets.length){ log(t('penalty.stasisLog',{n:targets.length}),'bad'); toast(t('penalty.stasisToast'),true); }
      }

      if (S.stormT>=15){
        S.stormT = 0;
        const targets = S.field.filter(Boolean).sort(()=>Math.random()-0.5).slice(0,3);
        targets.forEach(u=>{ u.stunT=Math.max(u.stunT||0,2); burst(u.x,u.y,'#3de0c8',10); });
        if (targets.length){ S.shake=Math.max(S.shake,6); log(t('penalty.stormLog',{n:targets.length}),'bad'); toast(t('penalty.stormToast'),true); }
```

```js
/* ---------------- penalty UI ---------------- */
function buildPenalties(){
  const grid=$('penaltyGrid'); grid.innerHTML='';
  const locked = S.mode!=='menu' && S.mode!=='lobby';
  const active = locked ? S.penalties : [...selectedPenalties];
  PENALTIES.forEach(p=>{
    const sel = active.includes(p.key);
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='penCard'+(sel?' sel':'')+(locked?' locked':'');
    b.dataset.key=p.key;
    b.innerHTML=`<i>${p.icon}</i><div><b>${esc(tl(p,'name'))}</b><span>${esc(tl(p,'desc'))}</span></div><em>${sel?'✔':''}</em>`;
    b.onclick=()=>togglePenalty(p.key);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  });
  $('penaltyCount').textContent=t('penalty.count',{n:active.length});
  $('penaltyReward').textContent=active.length>=5?t('penalty.xpBonus',{pct:100}):t('penalty.rewardNeed');
  $('penaltyReward').className=active.length>=5?'penReward on':'penReward';
  $('penaltyLockNote').textContent=locked?t('penalty.locked'):t('penalty.sub');
}
function syncPenaltyBtn(){
  const n = (S.mode==='menu'||S.mode==='lobby') ? selectedPenalties.size : S.penalties.length;
  const b=$('penaltyBtn');
  b.textContent=t('ctl.penalty')+(n?` ${n}`:'');
  b.classList.toggle('on',n>0);
}
function openPenalties(){ buildPenalties(); $('penaltyOverlay').classList.add('show'); }
```

/* ============ ANIME EXTRA RANDOM DEFENCE ============ */
:root{
  --bg:#0d0b1e; --bg2:#14102a; --surface:#1a1633; --surface2:#221c42; --overlay:#2b2352;
  --ink:#f2ead8; --ink-dim:#a89ec8; --ink-faint:#6f6596;
  --magenta:#ff3d8e; --cyan:#35e0ff; --gold:#ffc93d; --violet:#8b5cff;
  --success:#3dff9a; --warn:#ffc93d; --danger:#ff3d5e; --info:#35e0ff;
  --gE:#9fb4c8; --gD:#4aa8ff; --gC:#3dff9a; --gB:#ff9440; --gA:#ffd23d; --gS:#e14dff; --gEX:#ff3d5e; --gSTAR:#ffffff;
  --spirit:#7af0ff; --wisp:#c8a8ff;
  --line:#342a5e;
  --font:'Kanit','Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;
  --mono:'Cascadia Code','Consolas',monospace;
}
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{height:100%}
body{
  background:var(--bg); color:var(--ink); font-family:var(--font);
  overflow-x:hidden; min-height:100vh;
}
body::before{
  content:''; position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:-1; pointer-events:none;
...
  #diffRow{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);max-height:200px}
  #startFactionRow{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}
  #roomControls{flex-wrap:wrap}
  #endStats{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
}---
5:  --magenta:#ff3d8e; --cyan:#35e0ff; --gold:#ffc93d; --violet:#8b5cff;
6:  --success:#3dff9a; --warn:#ffc93d; --danger:#ff3d5e; --info:#35e0ff;
33:::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--violet),var(--magenta));border-radius:6px;border:2px solid var(--bg2)}
34:::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover{background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--cyan),var(--violet))}
35:*{scrollbar-width:thin;scrollbar-color:var(--violet) var(--bg2)}

#### Thinking

Let's analyze each one:

1. **PENALTIES data test**: "Values have same structure but are not reference-equal" — `assert.deepStrictEqual(pens.map(p=>p.key).sort(), [...PEN_KEYS].sort())` — vm context arrays have a different prototype (cross-realm), so deepStrictEqual fails on reference comparison of prototypes. Fix: use `assert.deepEqual` (loose comparison), or JSON.stringify both. Actually, node's deepStrictEqual checks prototypes across realms. Use `assert.deepStrictEqual(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(...)), ...)` or compare joined strings. I'll go with comparing `.join(',')`.

2. **raid** (line 74): `assert.strictEqual(raid.buildWave(10).length, plain.buildWave(10).length*2)` — buildWave(10): count = min(30, 8+5)=13 normally; raid: min(60, 13*2)=26. 26 = 13*2 ✓. Hmm, wait — Math.random=()=>0.5 affects type selection but not count. So lengths are 13 vs 26 ✓. Next, hp: plain's hp = round((10+4+0.5)*1*1*1*1)=round(14.5)=15? w=S.wave — startWave sets S.wave=1. (10+4*1+0.5*1)=14.5 → 15. raid: 14.5*0.5=7.25 → round=7. Test: `assert.strictEqual(b.maxHp, Math.round(a.maxHp*0.5))` → Math.round(15*0.5)=Math.round(7.5)=8 ≠ 7. Rounding mismatch! Fix the test: compute expected value from the formula, or compare with tolerance. Better approach: assert b.maxHp === Math.round(14.5*0.5)... but the test shouldn't hardcode it. Use `assert.ok(Math.abs(b.maxHp - a.maxHp*0.5) <= 1)`. Same for gold: plain's gold=round((3+0.7)*1*1)=round(3.7)=4; raid=round(3.7*0.5)=round(1.85)=2; Math.round(4*0.5)=2 ✓ gold passes, hp fails. Fix the hp assertion with tolerance ±1.

3. **powerfulLeader** (line 92): first.maxHp = round(14.5*20)=290; second=round(14.5)=15; 15*20=300 ≠ 290. Rounding again. Fix: tolerance, or compare with Math.round(second.maxHp... no wait. first = round(base*20), second=round(base). Use `assert.ok(Math.abs(first.maxHp - second.maxHp*20) <= 20)`. gold: first=round(3.7*10)=37, second=round(3.7)=4, 4*10=40≠37. Tolerance ≤10. Or, more cleanly: stub Math.random and compute the base formula inside the test... the simplest is a tolerance check.

4. **periodic**: stasis stuns fewer than 2 after 11 updates. Why? withPenalties() calls startGame, then sets S.penalties directly — but startGame sets stasisT/stormT based on hasPen at the time of startGame, which was [] → stasisT=0, stormT=0. So the first stasis fires at t=20, not t=10! After 11 updates: stasisT=11 <20 → doesn't fire. Storm fires at t=15 → not yet at t=11. So 0 units are stunned.

The withPenalties helper sets penalties after startGame — the timers aren't initialized. In actual usage, penalties are selected before startGame, so the timers are set correctly. The test helper should reflect that: select penalties via togglePenalty before startGame. Let's rewrite withPenalties:

```js
function withPenalties(keys, diff='practice'){
  const ctx = loadGame();
  keys.forEach(k => ctx.togglePenalty(k));
  ctx.startGame({ diffKey: diff });
  return ctx;
}
```

This is more faithful. togglePenalty works in menu mode ✓. Then startGame copies selectedPenalties and sets the timers ✓.

But wait — the XP test uses withPenalties and then addFactionXp — no problem. The toggle test uses loadGame directly — no problem.

Also, the 'penalties toggle' test asserts that togglePenalty returns false during play — withPenalties no longer sets S.penalties manually, but that's fine.

Let's also double-check the periodic timing with the correct timers: stasisT=10, stormT=12.
- update(1)×11: at t=3, storm fires (stormT 12+3=15 → fires, stormT=0, stuns 3 units for 2 seconds). Field loop in the same tick: those units get stunT-=1 → 1. At t=5, the storm's stun expires (1-1-1 → 0 at t=5... let's trace: stun is set to 2 at t=3, field loop subtracts 1 → 1; t=4: 1-1=0; t=5: no stun). At t=10, stasis fires (stasisT 10+10=20 → fires, stasisT=0, stuns 2 units for 3 seconds), field loop subtracts 1 → 2. t=11: 2-1=1 >0 ✓. After 11 updates: 2 units have stunT=1 ✓ >=2.
- update(1)×8 (t=12..19): stasis stun: t=12: 1-1=0. Storm: stormT=0 at t=3, then accumulates: fires at t=18 (stormT=15), stuns 3 units for 2 seconds, field loop subtracts 1 → 1. t=19: 1-1=0. Hmm! After the 19th update, the storm's stun = 0 → check fails!

Trace stormT: fires at t=3 → stormT=0. t=4..18: stormT=1..15 → fires at t=18 → stormT=0, stuns 3 units for 2 seconds; field loop in the same update subtracts 1 → 1. t=19: stunT=1-1=0. So after 8 more updates (t=19), stunned=0. Fails.

Fix: check after 7 more updates (t=18): storm just fired, stunT=1 → >=2 ✓ (3 targets). Change the second loop to 7. At t=18: stasis stun expired; storm stuns 3 units → after field loop, 1 remaining → 3 units >0 ✓.

Actually, wait — the order: the periodic block runs before the field loop. At t=18, storm sets stunT=2 on 3 units; field loop: stunned units get stunT-=1 → 1, return. So 3 units have stunT=1 ✓.

Let's set the second loop to `i<7`.

Also, the first loop: at t=11, stasis fired at t=10, stun 3 → after field loop at t=10: 2; t=11: 1. 2 units ✓.

Now let's fix the tests.

Four test issues: cross-realm array comparison, rounding tolerances, and the helper must select penalties before startGame (so timers initialize). Fixing the tests.
